→</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"x021c01","title":"A Clock without a Curse","volume":"婚后外传：The Dawn Bell","anchor":"chapter-0157","paragraphs":["Cassian woke to a small metal wheel resting on his chest. \"A wedding present?\" he asked. Mara, already dressed for the city science fair, answered with the unconditional affection of a woman about to volunteer her husband for trouble. \"A plea for help. You may wake up now. Someone has stolen the Dawn Bell.\" He tried to look virtuous and failed when she smiled.","At the fair, multiple pavilions straddled the river. Dr Sami Darzi, a specialist in nuclear engineering, had lent his grandfather's mechanical clock to an exhibition about models. Photographs from the Middle East showed its earlier home. Darzi's family practiced a religion in which the clock held no sacred status; the curator had nevertheless advertised a ritualistic dawn ceremony. That stereotype infuriated his daughter Leila. Her plea for a modest correction had disappeared beneath the publicity.","\"It's an instrument,\" she said. \"My grandfather's philosophy was that good machinery should wake lazy people.\"","Mara liked that standpoint. A boy beside the clock's empty pillar laughed, then asked whether the theft proved the family's superstition about bad luck. Leila's fingers tightened. What he called superstition was another curator's invention. Prejudice could be embedded in a display before anyone noticed. Cassian changed the subject to the wheel's observable wear, allowing Leila to regain her train of thought.","The museum had linked every pavilion to an algorithm that selected a visitor's route. Its artificial intelligence supposedly inferred interests from a poll. Mara's recommendation consisted of dentistry, twice, followed by an advertisement for expensive smiles. Cassian's included chemistry, biotechnology, and a dream interpretation stall. \"Our marital dynamics are becoming clearer,\" he said. \"Your teeth are in danger; my subconscious requires sponsorship.\"","Leila managed a smile. The fair's aesthetic had breadth: visualization screens could sit alongside handmade models. A sturdy bridge connected an economics game to a pavilion where a patchwork banner compared socialism with market systems. The pamphlet allowed multiple perspectives. The route program, however, directed almost everyone away from the public-service displays. The event's statistics then described the unpopular stands as proof of public preference.","The staff had been asked to coordinate thousands of visits without extra help. Trent praised his innovational route planner, whose suggestions could range from a public lecture to a private sales booth. The dentistry sponsor was notoriously eager to cater to wealthy clients. Mara chose to focus on the unpaid attendants instead. Their growing consciousness of the clock's disappearance might produce a better lead than the director's promises.","\"That is a remarkably convenient notion,\" Mara said. \"The stimulus comes from the system that measures the response.\"","Darzi had helped construct the original configuration. He explained the mechanics with an analogy rather than a lecture. A clock needed coordination between wheel and spring; the fair needed agreement between question, measurement, and conclusion. Relativity did not mean every subjective viewpoint was equivalent. A different perspective could reveal an aspect of the problem while still requiring a probe into the evidence. Calling every disagreement bias would merely replace one stereotype with another.","Leila wanted to contemplate a formal complaint, including the fair's misuse of her family's historical photographs. An attendant warned that challenging the director would cost her future invitations. Nonetheless, she had rights under the loan agreement. Darzi agreed to support her without trying to manipulate the wording himself.","The director, Basil Trent, arrived with an assurance that nothing valuable was at risk. His defensive attitude changed when Mara showed him a cut cable behind the pillar. It had once held the clock's safety strap. His cursory glance became an attempt to manipulate the timetable: the presentation would proceed, then the investigation. Mara refused that order. In retrospect, Darzi said, he had ignored a thorn in the arrangements because his sense of responsibility made him reluctant to burden a tired staff.","\"Someone offered an absolute guarantee,\" Leila said. \"You gave them acceptance because you wanted to believe it.\"","\"Self-deception,\" Darzi agreed quietly. \"Not my proudest experiment.\"","Mara followed a trail of grease to a photography tent. A bald operator stood before a representation of the clock. It was a live image, he insisted. Cassian touched the cold lighting unit. His skepticism was rewarded when the supposed second hand froze. The operator blamed a syndrome that made Leila \"hysterical\" whenever plans changed. Darzi's sympathy vanished. His daughter had a diagnosed fatigue syndrome; the insult was no substitute for an explanation.","Leila's perception of one tiny shadow opened the case. The image had been taken before the wheel was removed, and its reflection showed Trent's ring. Her virtuosity with photographs mattered more than the elaborate dream analysis the system had recommended. Mara felt the familiar pull of a guess, an unconscious eagerness to make the first plausible story fit. She stopped herself. Reductionism would make the director into a single bad trait before they knew where the clock had gone.","Beyond the tent, theatrical vapor began to pour across a walkway. A technician shouted that a valve had stuck. Cassian helped evacuate the nearest stall while Mara reached the control lever. Her athletic leap earned him a private, thoroughly inappropriate smile. The vapor was cool water, but the slippery deck was real. They helped a pregnant attendant adjust to a folding chair and called for the site's medic.","Mara found the clock's shipping label in the operator's pocket. He had planned to translate a brief removal into a harmless publicity stunt. \"Nobody was meant to mind,\" he said.","\"Unfairness becomes easier when you decide that in advance,\" Leila answered.","Trent was already crossing the bridge with a padded case. The fair's promised reinterpretation of public curiosity had become a question of reliance: who had trusted whom, and why? Mara handed the label to security. center. Trent had pressed a demonstration switch, intending to strand his pursuers above the water. The observable result was less impressive: his own case struck a pillar, and the stolen clock rang twelve times. Cassian, who had read the mechanical warning, caught Mara around the waist and lifted her onto the fixed platform. \"I believe we're due to wake up the whole fair,\" he said.","Leila arrived through the chemistry pavilion with her father's tool bag. She did not share his virtuosity in nuclear engineering, but she knew this clock's configuration. The spring would break if Trent kept shaking it. Her sense of responsibility overcame her sympathy for the man. She shouted the correct latch sequence. He obeyed, then claimed he had merely been carrying the instrument to safety.","\"A plausible notion,\" Mara said. \"If we disregard the false picture and the cut strap.\"","The operator had told security that Trent wanted multiple publicity incidents to improve the poll results. This did not establish every aspect of the scheme. Mara followed her own train of thought: staged excitement would bring visitors to a paid platform, whose algorithm would then steer them to sponsors. Reliance on the resulting statistics would make the preferences appear spontaneous. The unfairness was concealed inside the measurement.","Trent tried an analogy. \"A fair is like a shop window. It must cater to what people enjoy.\"","From Leila's standpoint, the insult was another expression of prejudice, a thorn that had grown too familiar. She had been trying to adjust to the fair's habits when she should have questioned them. The director could straddle art and commerce without making criticism taboo. His reinterpretation of her objection as illness had crossed a line.","\"Then let them choose,\" Leila said. \"Don't construct a choice and call its acceptance proof of demand.\"","The director's defensive attitude hardened. He called her hysterical again. This time her father spoke before Cassian could. \"Her fatigue syndrome is not an explanation for your conduct. Nor is your nervousness proof of guilt. Answer the actual question.\"","Mara admired the distinction. She had felt a subconscious temptation to regard his bald head and expensive ring as the portrait of a villain. That was a subjective association, not a reliable probe. The photograph's ring mattered because it matched his own, not because jewelry denoted dishonesty. Cassian quietly moved between Trent and the open stairs. His assurance needed no theatrical emphasis.","The route visualization on the platform still displayed cheerful arrows. From a visitor's viewpoint, the broken bridge looked open. Mara asked the operator to focus on that immediate hazard and change the map. He had hoped to translate excitement into acclaim; now he could embed a warning in the screen instead. The small repair was no equivalent for an apology, but it was a start.","Leila showed them her earlier correction request. It asked that the ritualistic ceremony invented for her family be removed. Religion and clockmaking could sit alongside each other without a stereotype pretending to explain their relationship. The photographs from the Middle East depicted one particular household, not a single philosophy shared by an entire region. Trent had called that breadth of explanation a burden on visitors' attention.","\"You wanted a stronger stimulus,\" she said. \"A little exotic superstition.\"","Darzi admitted that his dream of a successful fair had encouraged self-deception. He had treated long hours as virtuous without asking who could bear them. The recognition cost him more than a public speech would have done.","\"It was affectionate.\"","\"Your affection didn't require our acceptance.\"","Mara took the case when security arrived. Before she could set it down, a worker collapsed beside the platform. The young man was unconscious for several alarming seconds. The medic, already nearby, reached him first. Cassian cleared space while Leila brought water for later. When the man could answer, he described an overnight shift followed by a missed breakfast. The medic continued her assessment; nobody used a convenient story to replace it.","Darzi stared at the staff schedule. It was a patchwork of favors, an unfair load that his own enthusiasm had helped normalize. In retrospect, the fair's philosophy of tireless creativity had made exhaustion admirable. He would need to rethink his own contribution as well as Trent's. Mara appreciated that uncomfortable perspective. Reductionism could flatter the accuser as readily as it condemned the accused.","Trent protested that he had preserved the fair's aesthetic. The route program's artificial intelligence was innovational, he said, and his carefully staged incidents made education powerfully memorable. He pointed toward the economics display, where socialism was discussed under a banner about shared resources. Wasn't it virtuous to attract money for public learning?","\"You can reaffirm a good aim and still betray it,\" Cassian said. \"Your ends don't give you unconditional permission.\"","A sturdy rail separated them from the water. On its wet surface Mara saw grease at the height of a child's hands. Several children had crossed while the bridge was moving. The safety margin had been notoriously narrow even before Trent interfered. A cursory inspection should have caught that. The operator's frightened consciousness of the danger had arrived after his hope of a bonus; he began to cry.","The fair needed coordination, not another absolute promise. Leila asked the technician to disable the moving bridge and coordinate a fixed crossing. Darzi agreed to help with the dynamics after the inspector approved the plan. Across the river, the dentistry stall lent chairs, while a biotechnology team contributed dry towels. A shared sense of security emerged from specific tasks, not the director's rhetoric.","Mara checked the clock. Its visual representation had hidden a tiny bent pin, which Leila's photograph could illuminate better than memory. The tools might range from a camera to a screwdriver, but the problem remained concrete. Relativity had no bearing on the need for a sound spring. Cassian's skepticism toward a perfect solution made her grin.","\"You're enjoying this,\" he accused.","\"I'm enjoying you. There is a distinction.\"","The medic's patient sat up and offered a shaky thumbs-up. Leila returned his gesture. The clock was safe, the director detained, and the fair had a problem it could finally describe honestly. Paradoxically, that modest result improved Mara's perception of its future."],"questions":[],"wordCount":1046,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0158\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">婚后外传：The Dawn Bell</div><h1>The Rotating Bridge</h1><p id=\"chapter-0158-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1107\" data-words=\"1107\">bridge</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-896\" data-words=\"896\">began</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8538\" data-words=\"8538\">to</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7164\" data-words=\"7164\">rotate</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-892\" data-words=\"892\">before</a> Mara <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6752\" data-words=\"6752\">reached</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4571\" data-words=\"4571,4568\">its</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1340\" data-words=\"1340\">center</a>. clock on a low pillar. No ritualistic performance surrounded it. He told the children how his father had used it to wake up a family that preferred sleeping. The photographs from the Middle East remained, with a new caption naming the household and town. Religion appeared only where the family had chosen to discuss it. A stereotype had surrendered to a particular life.","Mara stood beside Cassian at the back. His hand rested on her waist with the comfortable, nearly unconscious confidence she had once thought belonged to other people's marriages. \"Your grandfather's philosophy,\" she whispered, \"would approve of the bell.\"","\"My grandfather would have hired someone else to rise early. A different standpoint.\" No sponsor would be allowed to manipulate the tally before publication.","Leila had proposed a new poll. Visitors could select multiple reasons for choosing a pavilion, including the route program's recommendation. They could also decline. The statistics would distinguish those routes from freely planned visits. The change could not remove every bias, but it would make one important stimulus observable. People who distrusted the project could inspect the questions before deciding whether to answer.","The inspector had closed the moving bridge pending repair. A fixed walkway now straddled the narrow river. Its central pillar supported the rail instead of a sponsor's enormous portrait. Darzi's nuclear engineering students had volunteered to carry barriers, but a local carpenter determined the fitting. Their virtuosity in one discipline did not give them absolute authority over another. Leila wrote that principle into the staff briefing without making it a public humiliation.","Trent's replacement wanted a dramatic reinterpretation of the fair as a triumph over deception. Mara suggested she rethink the slogan. A director could tell a hopeful story without erasing the worker who had been found unconscious beside the bridge. His fatigue had exposed an unfairness the theft alone could not explain. The sense of responsibility for his schedule belonged to several people, including Darzi, who had now paid the missing overtime from his own lecture fee while the institution arranged reimbursement.","The operator had sent Leila an apology. It called his description of her syndrome thoughtless, then admitted he had repeated it because he wanted to discredit her. That final sentence mattered. Sympathy for his fear did not require unconditional acceptance of his actions. She would speak to him after the hearing, when she was ready. Her father's hand tightened around his cup, but he respected the choice.","\"I wanted to protect her,\" he told Mara.","\"You are listening to her. Keep doing that.\"","The patchwork banner above the public-services tent had acquired a neat repair. Beneath it, two visitors debated socialism without treating the neighboring private firms as enemies. Their discussion was occasionally untidy. The fair's old notion that one smooth message would create agreement had been a form of self-deception. A public space could accommodate multiple viewpoints without pretending all claims had equal support.","Inside the models pavilion, a visualization of planetary motion drew a crowd. The presenter explained that perspective changed what viewers saw, while the underlying measurements still constrained the explanation. He avoided using relativity as permission for any belief whatsoever. A little girl raised a thorn of a question about the drawing's scale, and he welcomed it. Her perception of the tiny outer orbit had found a real weakness in his display.","The child wanted to probe the little model's hinge. Her subjective sense that it looked weak would need a real examination, and Darzi fetched a ruler. He chose to help her investigate rather than congratulate her on having already found the answer.","Mara watched Leila work beside the clock. Her father's habits had shaped her subconscious expectations: when a visitor frowned, she almost began to defend him. Then she asked what the visitor meant. He wanted to see the spring. His curiosity was no attack. The moment would never appear in a poll, yet it powerfully suggested a change in her reliance on first impressions.","Cassian had bought her a paper fan printed with a mechanical bird. \"For when your train of thought needs a little wind.\"","\"A modest contribution to my intellect.\"","\"I hoped you'd describe it as indispensable.\"","She kissed him in full view of a woman who promptly pretended to study a pulley. His delight was an excellent counterweight to the morning's earnestness.","Darzi asked them to inspect a final representation of the theft. He had made a small model, with the old bridge placed beside the route map. The juxtaposition let a physical failure sit alongside an institutional one. A broken latch could be repaired with tools; a culture of prejudice required people to change how they treated a correction. Reductionism would have made both problems the fault of one foolish man.","The new director asked whether skepticism would make the fair seem unreliable. \"Visitors want assurance,\" she said.","\"They also want a safe bridge,\" Mara replied.","The woman laughed, then wrote the repair date where everyone could see it. From her viewpoint, visible limits had seemed embarrassing. In retrospect, they were less costly than promises she could not keep. She would reaffirm the opening hours tomorrow only if the inspector approved them.","A child asked whether the clock carried bad luck. Leila answered without anger. \"That's superstition. It carries my grandfather's tool marks. Look here.\"","She guided his finger toward a scar on the brass. The old taboo against touching had protected the clock, but a supervised examination could teach another kind of care. The child leaned close. His face held no fear, only attention.","Paradoxically, the fair's most memorable display was now its least theatrical. Mara left with her husband's arm around her and a little metal bird turning in the breeze. Previous</button><button data-go=\"159\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"x022c01","title":"The Palace of Scent","volume":"婚后外传：Rain in a Bottle","anchor":"chapter-0160","paragraphs":["Six months after the wedding, Cassian lost his wife inside a cloud of orange blossom. The Palace of Scent offered visitors a visual map of perfume: curving paths, hanging glass, and water shallow enough to submerge a finger. At the moment of his entrance, Mara emerged from the mist wearing a borrowed silver cape. \"High fashion,\" she announced. \"The guide says you're allowed to adore me.\"","\"At last, permission.\"","He kissed her hand. A style-conscious visitor took a photograph of the cape, then recognized the woman wearing it and looked faintly disappointed. Mara enjoyed that more than admiration. Their marital holiday had survived an actual peaceful morning; she had no intention of wasting it by behaving with dignity.","The exhibition's creator, Etta Mer, brought them a rare bottle called Rain. Its essence had been developed by her late sister, a biologist who treated scent as both compound and personal inspiration. Etta's quest was to preserve the work without encouraging the misconception that every memory could be bottled. Her rigorous labels separated chemistry from imagination. A workshop on physiology examined sensitivity to odors, while a psychology researcher asked visitors how expectation shaped a rating. Nobody promised medical benefits.","A vendor outside sold ice-cream shaped like tiny perfume bottles. Mara thought the fad absurd and bought two. Etta's invitation catered to children as well as collectors; letting them play a major role in opening day set a welcome precedent. Cassian's delight in a melting dessert recalled a younger self she rarely saw in formal rooms.","\"Normally, curiosity pays the fare,\" Etta said. \"Today admission is for free. The banking industry sponsored the opening. Our demographic survey showed the old prices excluded half the town.\"","The museum's idealism had a practical framework. Its software contract permitted several kiosks under a site-licensing agreement, and the innovative display could operate without recording names. Etta's favorite programmer had recently decided to emigrate; his replacement wanted a grander appraisal of the system's intelligence. He proposed metrics based on how long visitors stood still. A student of sociology pointed out that a locked door could produce the same statistic. Etta had laughed, and the proposal had not survived.","Mara's instinct made her grasp the bottle firmly when the room shuddered. A low vibration traveled through the floor. A traveling actor, hired to demonstrate the old perfume trade, abandoned his speech. \"The moment of truth,\" he said, then stopped. Water rose above the first step of the model harbor.","At the intersection of two paths, a child began to cry. Cassian carried her to a dry platform while Mara followed the channel toward a control booth. The realistic storm display had continued beyond its marked limit. Continuing to wait would be foolish. An attendant tried to restart it, but the software announced that the site-licensing token had expired. An inability to display an image should not paralyze a physical safety control. The real emergency switch worked; an engineer stopped the pump.","Etta's preoccupation was the collection, but she asked about the visitors first. That choice gave Mara faith in her. The museum director's obsession with perfect appearances could have swallowed the afternoon. Instead, staff checked the shallow pools and moved the public calmly. Mara unconsciously tightened her grip on Rain before passing it to the conservator.","The storm machine was a prototype, Etta explained, adapted from a teaching model about geology and rainfall. Its mathematics had been checked, its physics tested with a robust overflow. The power failure should not have been possible under those conditions. Someone had covered the drain with a cloth. The acting water engineer, who normally worked at a nuclear station, knelt beside it. \"This isn't complicated science,\" she said. \"It's a blocked hole.\"","The traveling actor, Lio Ferr, claimed he had never entered the booth. His inability to look at the wet cape struck Mara as a peculiarity, although not a justification for accusing him. Her intuition needed something more reliable than embarrassment. She asked to see the imaging record used for the projected harbor. A sharp camera angle revealed his sleeve beside the drain fifteen minutes earlier.","\"You can destroy a reputation with one picture,\" he said.","The staff had practiced this route, so imagination did not have to do every job. Their reasoning followed the actual layout, while a constant bell guided visitors through the mist. The calm response was an example worth remembering. The ideal opening had vanished; this reality mattered more than a perfect photograph.","\"Or rescue one by examining the whole sequence,\" she answered.","Etta offered him a towel. The gesture might waken his trust, but he threw it down. \"She took my sister's work. There's your technique. Favoritism, dressed as wisdom.\"","The accusation transformed the affair. Mara's focus shifted from sabotage to a possible grievance. He held a letter alleging that Etta's sister had copied a smaller workshop's formula. The germ of the dispute lay in a payment made years ago. Etta looked honestly bewildered. Her conviction that all the records were clear had become disbelief.","Cassian found that Rain's padded tray now held an empty bottle. Mara had passed the original to a man in a conservator's coat. The man was gone. It was a rare moment when she wanted to kick herself rather than a door.","\"A distraction,\" she said. \"Lio's dramatic flair was practically an invitation to watch him.\"","Etta's vision of a generous opening might have been naive, but the deceit gave nobody an exception to the law. Mara took the accusation seriously and asked for the real conservator's help. Biology and arithmetic could wait; the geography of the exit mattered now.","The curving service stairs offered a short route to the quay. Cassian caught her cape before it snagged. \"Your strategy?\"","\"Get the bottle back. Then discover whose sister we owe an explanation.\"","\"An excellent tactic.\"","Mara grinned despite herself. Even so, she gave Etta the cape. \"Augment the guard at the doors. And please don't lose faith in free admission because someone abused it.\"","They ran into the mist, their peaceful morning conclusively over."],"questions":[],"wordCount":1012,"html":"<article The justification had the neat shape of a lie designed for somebody else's pain.","Mara could grasp the bottle if she got within a pace. Instead, she sat on the stone edge and examined the disputed letter's image on Lio's phone. The actor had followed them. His imagination had carried him through the dramatic opening; reality was proving harder. The letter's essence was a claim about ownership. It included a payment receipt, yet the recipient's name was partly obscured. A technique that cropped away an inconvenient line was no foundation for reasoning.","Rafe called her naive. \"The banking industry protects insiders. So does science. You expect a fair appraisal?\"","\"I expect the uncropped record,\" Mara said.","The traveling actor looked at the full receipt. Its numbers gave him no inspiration, only a tight feeling in his throat.
